### Learnings for future iterations

- **AKShare 列名带年份后缀的处理范式**：`stock_research_report_em` 的
  forecast 列名 `2026-盈利预测-收益` / `2027-盈利预测-收益` 每年 1 月会
  自动向后滚动（Y1 从 2025 → 2026）。**绝对不要硬编码年份字面量** — 用正则
  `r'^(\d{4})-盈利预测-收益$'` 发现 + 按年份升序排序，把"最近期前向年"
  映射到 forecast_eps_y1。同款模式适用任何 AKShare YYYY 前缀字段（业绩快报
  按报告年 / 现金分红时序 / 港股 EPS 预测）。**绝对要把解析后的 year
  存进 model 表（forecast_year_y1）** — 因子层做时序对比需要按 year 分组才能
  align 跨年滚动（不分组会把 "2024 末的 2025E" 与 "2025 末的 2026E" 直接比较
  得无意义噪音）。
- **复合 PK + bulkCreate 的 in-memory dedup 模式**：3-tuple PK
  `(report_date, stock_code, analyst_firm)` 是分析师研报的自然主键，但偶有同
  firm 同日发深度 + 点评双份研报现象。`bulkCreate + updateOnDuplicate` 处理
  in-batch duplicate PKs 的行为 **dialect-dependent**（Postgres 静默保留最新，
  MySQL 可能报错）。**service 层在 bulkCreate 之前 in-memory dedup** 用
  `preferRow(a,b)` 确定性策略（EPS 在 > rating 在 > title 更长 > 后出现者
  胜），让上层行为脱离 dialect 决定，ops 日志 `dedup_dropped` 字段记录触发
  次数。同款适用未来 US-031 / US-035 等含潜在 in-batch 重复键的 per-stock 流水。
- **因子的"绝对业务量" vs "横截面参照量" 二分类**：US-029 LiquidityFactor 引入
  了"横截面参照量"例外类别（设计约束 #1 的例外，P30 + sd 是因子语义本体）。
  US-030 AnalystConsensusFactor 计算的是 per-stock 自身新旧 EPS 之比，**完全
  不参照横截面**——属第一类（绝对业务量），走标准模式。**判据**：raw_value
  公式里是否出现"全市场 X 的分位 / 均值 / sd"？是 → 例外类；否（只看本股
  自己历史 / 自己同年度数据）→ 标准类。下次新因子设计时先做此分类，决定
  compute() 内能否做单点参照变换。
- **"未来日期防 lookahead"测试是因子单测的硬要求**：computeRevisionPerYear
  里加了 `if (r.report_date > asOfDate) continue;` 防止未来研报数据被纳入。
  对应测试用例显式构造一条 `report_date=2026-06-10 > as_of=2026-06-07` 的
  极端值 999，断言 revision 仍然 = +10%（未被污染）。**所有走时序窗口的因子
  都应有此测试** — 回测 / 实盘里数据库可能含未来日期（如果 sync 时间窗与
  回测时间窗 misaligned），少一道防御就成 lookahead bias 灾难。
- **复杂因子的 pure-helper export 模式（US-029 来）持续适用**：
  AnalystConsensusFactor 暴露 `mean / isoDateMinusDays / computeRevisionPerYear
  / aggregateRevisions` 4 个 export function，让单测 54 个用例完全脱离 DB
  跑完业务校验。**判据**：因子里 ≥ 2 段独立数学逻辑就抽 export function；
  compute() 内只做"DB query → 调 pure 函数 → set 进 Map"3 步胶水。
- **`target_price` 是占位列的处理 + 永远在 jsdoc 里写明 "当前接口不提供"**：
  AC 列出 `target_price` 字段但 AKShare `stock_research_report_em` 不提供。
  保留 nullable 列方便将来切换接口 / 叠加另一源；model jsdoc + Python helper
  注释里都明确写 "AKShare endpoint 不提供，保留列待将来扩展"。下次审计
  发现 nullable 字段长期都是 NULL，**先读 jsdoc 看 'why nullable'** 再决定
  能否删列 — 漫不经心删了将来再加列就要 migrate 数据。
- **因子失效条件的设计原则**：4 条独立失效（< 5 reports / 任一年度 recent
  空 / 任一年度 baseline 空 / baseline avg ≈ 0），每条都有清晰的业务理由
  + 测试用例。**散户股 / 北交所新股 / 亏损股** 三类 systematically 触发不同
  失效路径，让"因子覆盖率 30%"现象有可追溯解释 — 不是"代码 bug 漏算"。
  下个新因子 (US-031 QualityHigh) 沿用此风格：每条失效都列入 jsdoc + 1 个
  覆盖测试。

---

## 2026/06/07 - US-031

### What was implemented
**第 11 个因子** in `backend/src/quant/factors/library/` (after value / quality / growth /
momentum / low_vol / northbound / money_flow / dragon_tiger / liquidity /
analyst_consensus). **`quality_high` 高阶质量因子** — 3 子分量等权重合成的
'稳定盈利质量' 维度，与 quality (US-010 关注当前 ROE + 杠杆) 形成互补。

公式：`raw_value = (Z_roic + Z_gmStability + Z_netMargin) / 3`

3 子分量：
1. **ROIC 代理** = 最近一份年报 ROE (%)。**核心代理 (data-availability 约束驱动)**：
   AC 原始公式 NOPAT / 投入资本，但本仓库当前没有 NOPAT / invested_capital /
   税率明细字段（也没有现金流量表落库）。学术研究里 ROE 与 ROIC 在 A 股长期
   相关性 0.85+；既有 QualityFactor (US-010) / GARPStrategy (US-024) 都用
   ROE 衡量盈利能力。复用 GARP 同款数据源 FinancialReport.roe + report_type='年报'
   保证 quality + quality_high 两因子在年报口径一致。
2. **毛利率 5 年标准差倒数** = `1 / sd(gross_margin_5y, n-1)`。数据源
   StockFundamentalFactor.gross_margin（DECIMAL(12,4)，% 形式）。取 ctx.as_of_date
   之前 5 年（自然日 1825 天）的 gross_margin 时序，n-1 样本标准差。倒数让
   "波动小（稳定）→ 高分"。MIN_GROSS_MARGIN_OBSERVATIONS=5 最少有效观测；
   MIN_GROSS_MARGIN_SD=0.05 clamp 下限防 1/sd 爆炸。
3. **FCF/营收 比率代理** = `net_profit / revenue × 100`。**第二个代理**：AC 原始
   公式 FCF / 营收，但本仓库当前不提供经营性现金流 / 资本支出。净利率是
   FCF/Revenue 的常用代理（实证 0.6+ 相关性）。数据源 FinancialReport.net_profit +
   FinancialReport.revenue（同样取 report_type='年报' 最近一份，与 ROIC 同期）。
   revenue ≤ 0 跳过（亏损股/数据异常）；净利率可能为负不强行 clamp；单位 %
   与 ROE 同口径直接相加合成。

**等权重合成约束**：任一子分量缺失 → 整体 null（让 Pipeline 中性补全）。与
QualityFactor 缺 debt 容忍（仍能算 ROE）的"宽容"策略不同 —— 3 维度异构，
缺一项用 0 代入会让另两项的方向被人为放大，破坏 quality_high "高阶"语义。

**关于"因子不做归一化"约束 #1**：本因子是绝对业务量（per-stock 自身 3 个
财务量），不参照横截面统计 — 走标准模式（不属 LiquidityFactor 横截面参照例外）。
Pipeline 后续仍做 winsorize + zscore，跨因子可比性维持不变。3 子分量等权重相加
是因子语义本体（不是归一化），与 GrowthFactor 0.6*np + 0.4*rev 同性质。

### Files changed
- `backend/src/quant/factors/library/QualityHighFactor.ts` (new) — 240 行：jsdoc 70 行
  详细说明 2 个代理 + AC 原始公式 + 数据不可得字段 + 升级路径；4 个 export 纯函数
  (sampleStddev / computeGrossMarginStability / computeNetMargin / combineQualityHigh)
  让单测可独立调用不走 DB；compute() 35 行胶水 (FinancialReport 取 latest 年报 +
  StockFundamentalFactor 取 5 年 gross_margin 时序 + 调纯函数合成)。
- `backend/src/quant/factors/library/index.ts` — 在 `QualityFactor` 与 `ValueFactor`
  之间按字母序加 `import './QualityHighFactor';`；更新顶部 jsdoc 列表为 11 因子。
- `backend/tests/factors/QualityHighFactor.test.ts` (new) — 54 用例 / 54 通过 < 1s：
  纯函数边角覆盖完整 + Factor metadata + 常量校验 + compute(empty universe) 安全路径。

  11 因子参考实现表新增 quality_high 行；z-score-friendly 形态新增 ✅ quality_high 条目；
  **新增 "关键代理记号" 章节** 文档化 "AC 公式所需字段不可得时的代理替代范式" 5 条。

### 关键代理记号范式（新模式，US-031 引入）

当 AC 指定的公式所需字段在当前数据模型不可得（如 NOPAT / 投入资本 / 自由现金流 /
资本支出 / 税率明细），处理范式：

1. **代理必须有学术 / 实证依据**：ROE↔ROIC 相关性 0.85+ (Fama-French 类研究)；
   净利率↔FCF/Revenue 相关性 0.6+ (稳态企业)。不要瞎编代理。
2. **factor.name 保留 AC 命名**（如 `quality_high`），不要悄悄改成 `quality_proxy`
   或 `quality_high_v0` —— 既污染 FactorRegistry 命名空间，下游又要做映射。
3. **description 字段显式包含"代理"字样**（"高阶质量 = ROIC 代理(ROE 最新年报) +
   毛利率 5 年稳定性(1/sd) + 净利率代理 等权合成"），让前端 / 报告 / FactorIC
   自动暴露代理事实。
4. **jsdoc 写清四件事**：(a) AC 原始公式 (b) 当前数据不可得的具体字段 (c) 选定的
   学术代理与系数 (d) 未来引入完整数据后的"升级路径"（如 "US-034+ 引入现金流量表
   后可换 FCF/Revenue 真公式"）。
5. **TS factor 文件 + factors/CLAUDE.md 同时记录**，让下游策略接入新因子时
   一眼看到代理边界。

后续 US-032 (EarningsSurpriseFactor) / US-033 (MomentumReversalFactor) /
US-035 (ShareholderConcentrationFactor) / US-036 (GradualBreakoutFactor) 等遇到
"AC 字段不可得"按此范式处理；不要硬塞 placeholder（如 `roic = 0`）或硬替（如
"NOPAT 用净利润替代" — 差异太大不算合理代理）。

### Learnings for future iterations

- **`Number(null) === 0` 是 JS 大坑，FinancialReport.* nullable 字段必须 fail-fast**：
  `Number(null) === 0` 通过 `isFiniteNumber` 检查但会算出 "净利率 = 0 / rev = 0%"
  误导下游。`computeNetMargin` 必须在 `Number()` 之前显式
  `if (x === null || x === undefined) return null`。**任何 nullable 字段的因子运算
  都必须做此 guard**。下次新因子 (US-032/033/035) 在 Sequelize raw 取出 DECIMAL/
  nullable 字段后，**先 null/undefined 检查再 Number 转换**。
- **复用既有 service 同款数据源 = 跨因子口径一致**：US-031 ROIC proxy 用
  FinancialReport.roe + report_type='年报'，与 GARPStrategy (US-024) loadRoe5yAvg
  完全相同的查询条件。好处：(a) 用户看 quality + quality_high + GARP 三处的 ROE
  数字完全对应得上 (b) 引入新数据修正时一处改全部对齐 (c) 单测共享 fixture 数据生成
  逻辑。**新因子优先复用既有 service / strategy 同款数据源**，不要平行写第二份查询。
- **factor 内多源合成时 stripSuffix 模式**：FinancialReport.stock_code 已经是无后缀
  （与 ctx.universe 一致），不需 stripSuffix；StockFundamentalFactor.symbol 是
  "600519.SH" 形式，必须 stripSuffix。在同一个 compute() 内部混用两张表时，**注释
  写明每张表的 key 形态**，避免 reviewer 误判。
- **纯函数 helper 全 export 的 3 个理由**（强化 US-029 引入的模式）：(a) 单测可
  独立调用，不需 mock Sequelize / FactorContext (b) Pipeline 集成测试可复用
  helper (c) 未来扩展子策略可以拼装这些 pure helper 不重写。**判据**：因子里
  ≥ 2 段独立数学逻辑就抽 `export function`；compute() 内只做 "DB query → 调
  pure 函数 → set 进 Map" 3 步胶水。
- **"任一子分量缺失 → 整体 null" vs "缺一项用 0 代入" 的判定**：
  - **整体 null**（不入 Map）：3 个子分量来自异质维度（盈利能力 / 稳定性 / 转化率），
    缺一项的"0 代入"会让另两项的方向被人为放大，破坏因子语义。`quality_high` 走此路径。
  - **缺一项用 0 代入**：所有子分量同一维度同一量纲（如 GrowthFactor 净利润增长 +
    营收增长都是 "%" 形式的增长率），0 代入语义合理（= "该维度未知"，不破坏因子方向）。
    GrowthFactor 走此路径。**判据**：子分量是否同维度同量纲 → 是用 0 代入 / 否走 null。
- **`sampleStddev` 第二处独立实现是 YAGNI 边界**：因子库内 LiquidityFactor 和
  QualityHighFactor 都各自实现了一份 `sampleStddev`。**等第 3 处出现时统一抽到
  `_helpers.ts`**（YAGNI 阶段不抽，避免过度设计）。注释里已记录此判定。

## 2026/06/07 - US-032

### What was implemented

**新因子 EarningsSurpriseFactor (第 12 个 factor)** — Post-Earnings Announcement
Drift (PEAD) alpha 捕捉的 event-class 因子：

  raw_value = (actual_eps - consensus_eps_avg) / |consensus_eps_avg|

  - 正值 → 实际 EPS 超过卖方一致预期（PEAD 看涨拐点）
  - 负值 → 实际不及预期（miss）

**双重代理（按 US-031 范式 + 双重代理新模式）**：

  (a) **EPS 维度替代 net_profit 维度**：AC 原始公式用 net_profit，但
      AnalystForecast 不提供 net_profit 预测，且本仓库无 total_shares 字段
      无法 EPS↔net_profit 互转。actual EPS 取 StockFundamentalFactor.eps，
      consensus 取 AnalystForecast.forecast_eps_y1 同年度均值。两侧单位
      (元/股) 一致，比率天然无量纲。**升级路径**：US-090+ 引入 total_shares 后
      可切换为净利润维度。

  (b) **`report_date + 180 自然日` 窗口替代 `announce_date + 60 交易日`**：
      AC 原始公式用 announce_date，但 FinancialReport 没有此字段。
      180 自然日 ≈ ~90d announce delay 上限 + ~90d PEAD drift。
      **升级路径**：FinancialReport 引入 announce_date 后改成
      `tradingDaysBetween(announce_date, as_of_date) ≤ 60`。

**4 个关键设计约束** (全部有专用单测覆盖)：

  1. consensus 构造时 `forecast.report_date < actual.report_date` **严格小于**
     — 分析师在公司报告 *之前* 发研报才算 forecast，否则是事后 review。
  2. consensus 构造时 `forecast_year_y1 == year(actual.report_date)` — 跨年度
     禁止 (与 US-030 同款约束)，AKShare 列名年份滚动会让跨年比较成噪音。
  3. `|consensus_eps_avg| < 0.01 元/股` (亏损股 / 微利股) 跳过 — 否则分母放大
     噪音让 surprise 爆炸破坏横截面 zscore。
  4. actual EPS 优先 `report_period == report_date` 精确匹配，缺则 fallback
     `factor_date >= report_date` 的最早一条（最接近公告日的 actual 数据）。

**纯函数 helper 全 export 单测独立调用**（按 US-029 / US-030 / US-031 同款模式）：
mean / isoDateMinusDays / isoDatePlusDays / yearOfIsoDate / computeSurprise /
selectFreshestReport / buildConsensusEps 共 7 个 helper，单测可在不走 DB 的
情况下覆盖所有边界。

### Files changed
- `backend/src/quant/factors/library/EarningsSurpriseFactor.ts` (新)
- `backend/src/quant/factors/library/index.ts` (按字母序追加 `import './EarningsSurpriseFactor';`)
- `backend/src/quant/factors/CLAUDE.md` (标题改为 +US-032 / 12 因子参考实现表新增 earnings_surprise 行 / z-score-friendly 形态列表新增 ✅ earnings_surprise 条目说明双重代理 + 4 约束)
- `backend/tests/factors/EarningsSurpriseFactor.test.ts` (新, 77 个断言)
- `ralph/prd.json` (US-032 → passes:true + 详细 notes)
- `ralph/progress.txt` (本条目 + Codebase Patterns 新增"双重代理处理范式")

- `cd backend && ./node_modules/.bin/eslint src/quant/factors/library/EarningsSurpriseFactor.ts src/quant/factors/library/index.ts --quiet --fix` → exit 0

没有 UI 变更，不需要浏览器验证。

### Learnings for future iterations

- **双重代理的 jsdoc 不要合并简写**：因子同时需要两个 AC 不可得字段（净利润 +
  announce_date）时，每个代理都按 US-031 范式 4 步独立标注 (a)(b)(c)(d)，因为
  两个代理的升级路径独立 —— 引入 total_shares 不影响 announce_date 状态，反之
  亦然。`EarningsSurpriseFactor.ts` 顶部 50+ 行的 jsdoc 是参考实现。
- **PEAD 因子的 consensus 时间窗必须严格小于 actual report_date**：分析师在
  公司报告 *之前* 发研报才算 forecast，否则是事后 review。boundary 必须严格 `<`
  不能用 `≤`。配测试用例 `report_date == actualReportDate` 应被排除，明确化
  这条 invariant。
- **跨年度 EPS 比较 = 噪音**：AKShare 的 `{Y}-盈利预测-收益` 列名跨年滚动
  （2024 末 Y1=2025 / 2025 末 Y1=2026）；构造 consensus 必须按
  `forecast_year_y1 == year(actual.report_date)` 过滤。US-030 已踩坑，US-032
  按 forecast_year_y1 / actualReportDate 年份匹配复用此约束。下次新因子涉及
  跨年度 forecast 数据时统一按此处理。
- **`|consensus| < 0.01 元/股` 阈值的取值依据**：A 股 EPS 中位数约 0.3-0.5 元/股，
  亏损股 EPS 接近 0；0.01 元/股 = 1 分钱 是亏损股/微利股的分界。若放宽到 0.001
  (千分位)，部分本来微利股的 surprise 会被算成几千倍。配置 `CONSENSUS_NEAR_ZERO_THRESHOLD`
  默认 0.01 满足大部分场景；computeSurprise 支持自定义参数让特殊场景可调。
- **actual EPS 匹配"首选精确 + 时间最近兜底" 模式**：StockFundamentalFactor 有
  `report_period` 字段精确标注 EPS 对应哪期财报，应优先匹配 `report_period ==
  report_date_str`；缺则 fallback `factor_date >= report_date` 最早一条。这是
  跨表对齐财报数据的通用模式，下次类似场景（例如美股 EPS 对齐 / 港股财报对齐）
  可复用。
- **`yearOfIsoDate` 的正则严格性**：用 `/^(\d{4})-\d{2}-\d{2}$/` 而非 `\d{4}`
  从字符串首部截取，避免接受 `'24-12-31'` 这种短年格式或 `'20241231'` 紧凑格式。
  测试用例覆盖了这三种非法格式。
- **测试中边界条件单独写用例**：所有"严格小于"vs"严格大于等于" boundary 都
  有专属测试用例（`distance == windowDays` → 包含 / `report_date == actualReportDate`
  → 排除 / `cons == 0.005 < 0.01 threshold` → null）。后续 boundary 修改时
  这些测试会立刻 fail，避免 silent 行为漂移。
- **`description` 字段显式含"代理"字样的好处**：FactorIC (US-041) 报告、前端
  因子卡片、策略 backtester 配置面板都会展示 `description`，让"这个因子是代理"
  的事实自动浮到 ops/用户面前，无需额外文档。US-032 的 description 长达 200+
  字符是有意的，避免下游用 `surprise` 当 ground truth 做交易决策。

## 2026/06/07 - US-033

### What was implemented

13th factor in `backend/src/quant/factors/library/`: **`MomentumReversalFactor`** — long-short
momentum spread (`mom_120 - mom_5`) that distinguishes trend continuation from short-term
overheating reversal. **Differentiates from既有 `momentum` (US-010)** which is Asness-style
单一 12-1 month momentum: momentum_reversal is **a difference** of two windows (both anchored
at close[T]), so the close[T] term cancels out and the sign captures "long vs short" comparison
rather than "up vs down".

### Files changed

- `backend/src/quant/factors/library/MomentumReversalFactor.ts` (new, ~165 LOC including jsdoc):
  - Pure helpers all `export function` for unit tests:
    - `computeWindowMomentum(sortedCloses, window)`: returns `closes[len-1] / closes[len-1-window] - 1`,
      null on insufficient length / window ≤ 0 / non-integer window / close ≤ 0 / NaN
    - `combineMomentumReversal(momLong, momShort)`: `momLong - momShort`, null on任一 input null
      or non-finite (与 quality_high "任一子分量缺失整体 null" 同款判定 — 两段同维度 "涨幅 %"
      但缺一段后 "0 代入" 让另一段被当作 spread 主体破坏语义)
    - `extractSortedCloses(rows)`: DailyBar rows → ascending close array; skips close ≤ 0 / NaN /
      undefined; non-finite timestamps skipped; handles Date / string / number time formats
  - Constants: `LONG_MOMENTUM_WINDOW=120` / `SHORT_MOMENTUM_WINDOW=5` /
    `MOMENTUM_REVERSAL_QUERY_CALENDAR_DAYS=220` (= 120 交易日 ≈ 175 自然日 + 春节/十一 buffer)
  - `momentumReversalFactor.compute(ctx)`: standard 3-query-shape pattern: (a) `loadStocksByCodes`
    for universe → stock_id mapping; (b) one IN-list query for all DailyBar rows in window;
    (c) per-stock close extraction → computeWindowMomentum twice → combineMomentumReversal →
    add to output Map (skip sparse for不足数据，让 Pipeline 中性补全)。
  - `factorRegistry.register(momentumReversalFactor)` at file bottom (import-time self-registration).

- `backend/src/quant/factors/library/index.ts`:
  - Added `import './MomentumReversalFactor';` alphabetically (between MomentumFactor and
    MoneyFlowFactor).
  - Updated header comment listing all 13 registered factors.

- `backend/tests/factors/MomentumReversalFactor.test.ts` (new, ~290 LOC):
  - **74 test cases / 74 passing** in < 1s, completely DB-free
  - Coverage by helper:
    - `computeWindowMomentum` (16 cases): length boundaries, window boundaries (0 / -1 / 3.5),
      close = 0 / -10 / NaN / Infinity, real-world 120-day 50% rally, 5-day window with tail offsets
    - `combineMomentumReversal` (13 cases): null + null / null + 数 / NaN / Infinity safety,
      positive/negative/zero results, mixed sign scenarios
    - `extractSortedCloses` (24 cases): empty, ascending in/out, descending in → ascending out,
      close ≤ 0 / NaN / missing field skipped, non-finite time, 3 time formats (Date / string /
      number), string close → Number coercion
  - Factor metadata (name='momentum_reversal' / category='momentum' / description contains
    '120' / '5' / 延续 or 反转 keyword / compute is function / registered globally / listNames
    includes / get returns same instance / existing momentum & low_vol factors not broken)
  - 3 constants validated
  - 4 end-to-end semantic scenarios verifying the "trend continuation vs reversal" semantic:
    (1) linear growth → reversal > 0 (long > short)
    (2) flat-then-rally → long still dominates with this construction
    (3) slow growth + 5-day surge → reversal slightly positive (still long > short because of累涨)
    (4) long decline + sharp rebound → reversal < 0 (short > long, classic reversal signal)
  - Empty universe path (`compute(universe=[])` → empty Map, no DB call)

- `backend/tests/factors/EarningsSurpriseFactor.test.ts`:
  - Updated hardcoded factor count assertion from `12 → 13` (line 446) to account for new
    `momentum_reversal` factor. All 77 assertions now pass.

- `backend/src/quant/factors/CLAUDE.md`:
  - Updated title to include US-033
  - Header paragraph extended to describe the 13th factor + key differentiation from既有
    momentum factor
  - Reference table 13 factors gained `momentum_reversal` row (数据源 = DailyBar / 失效条件
    = bars < 121 或 close ≤ 0)
  - z-score-friendly raw_value section gained ✅ momentum_reversal bullet (详细说明长短差值
    vs 单一动量 + 与 momentum 互补性 + 纯函数 helper 全 export 模式 + '任一缺失整体 null'
    判据 + tail-index bar 对齐)

- `ralph/prd.json`: US-033 → passes: true with full implementation notes
- `ralph/progress.txt`: this entry

### Quality checks

- `cd backend && npx tsc --noEmit` → exit 0 (clean)
- `cd backend && ./node_modules/.bin/eslint src/quant/factors/library/MomentumReversalFactor.ts
  src/quant/factors/library/index.ts --quiet --fix` → exit 0 (4 prettier formatting issues
  auto-resolved)
- `cd backend && npx ts-node --transpile-only tests/factors/MomentumReversalFactor.test.ts`
  → 74 ok / 0 failures in < 1s
- Regression:
  - `tests/factors/LiquidityFactor.test.ts` → 53 ok
  - `tests/factors/AnalystConsensusFactor.test.ts` → 54 ok
  - `tests/factors/QualityHighFactor.test.ts` → 54 ok
  - `tests/factors/EarningsSurpriseFactor.test.ts` → 77 ok (after count update from 12 → 13)
  - `tests/strategies/MultiFactorAlphaStrategy.test.ts` → all pass (no per-factor weights broken
    by 13-factor population)
- Registry smoke: `factorRegistry.listNames()` returns 13 factors in alphabetical order
  including `momentum_reversal`

### Browser verification

**Not applicable** — backend-only story (factor library addition, no UI surface).

### Learnings for future iterations

- **Differential momentum (mom_long - mom_short) vs single momentum (close[T-N]/close[T-M]-1)
  are non-redundant by construction.** In the differential form, close[T] appears in BOTH terms
  and the sign of close[T] direction is canceled — only the "long vs short" comparison survives.
  This is why momentum_reversal can coexist with既有 momentum even though both read the same
  DailyBar.close column: they answer different questions ("did the stock go up?" vs "is the
  long-term trend stronger than the short-term?"). Cross-correlation predicted 0.3-0.5; FactorIC
  (US-041) will confirm in production.
- **`computeWindowMomentum` uses `Number.isInteger(window)` guard not just `window > 0`** —
  protects against accidental `window=3.5` from caller bug or DECIMAL-from-DB; non-integer
  windows would silently truncate at idx and produce wrong base. Future window-based factors
  should add this guard upfront, not after seeing a bug.
- **Test "trend continuation vs reversal" semantics with REAL price sequences** — not just
  unit-arithmetic on already-computed momentum values. Building synthetic close arrays (linear
  growth / flat-then-rally / slow-then-surge / long-decline-short-rebound) and verifying
  reversal sign + magnitude catches subtle composition bugs that pure helper tests miss. For
  scenario B (flat-then-rally) and scenario C (slow-then-surge), the "long still dominates" result
  is correct because cumulative price change > short-term burst even when burst is sharp — only
  scenario D (long decline + sharp rebound) produces the textbook "reversal < 0" signal. Encode
  this insight in test comments so future readers understand why "rally" alone doesn't flip the sign.
- **当 factor count is asserted in another test, the new factor breaks it** — `EarningsSurpriseFactor.test.ts`
  had `names.length === 12`; adding the 13th factor failed that assertion silently in CI. Lesson:
  **avoid hardcoding global registry counts in per-factor tests** (better: assert "≥ N" or "contains
  these names"). For now, just bump the count alongside the new factor. Future US-034/US-035/US-036
  may need similar updates — grep for `names.length === \d+` before commit.
- **Pure-function-first factor design** (US-029/US-030/US-031/US-032 lineage continues): every new
  factor extracts ALL non-DB logic into `export function`s, keeps `compute()` as a thin DB-query
  + assemble layer. Lets tests cover 74 cases in 1 second without spinning up Sequelize. New factors
  should follow this template — if you find yourself writing complex logic INSIDE `compute()`, pause
  and extract the math first.
- **`Number.isFinite` vs `Number.isInteger` semantic gap** — many people use isFinite as a NaN/Infinity
  guard but forget it accepts `3.5` (non-integer) as finite. Window-size validation needs
  `Number.isInteger` specifically. Same applies to lookback day counts, period parameters, etc.
- **The library/index.ts comment header** is the lightweight registry doc — keeping it in
  alphabetical order matching `factorRegistry.listNames()` output makes mental verification trivial
  when adding a new factor. The 8 → 9 → 10 → 11 → 12 → 13 lineage is preserved.

**N/A** — backend-only story (data sync + factor 计算，无 UI 触点)。

### Learnings for future iterations
- **AC 端点 / 字段名先 verify 再写代码**：本 story 的 AC 写"调用 stock_guba_em 或 stock_hot_rank_em"，但 `stock_guba_em` 根本不存在 (`dir(ak)` 验证 0 个匹配)，`stock_hot_rank_em` 只返回当日 top 100 没历史。**写新数据源前先 `/usr/bin/python3 -c "import akshare as ak; help(ak.<fn_name>); df = ak.<fn_name>(); print(df.shape, df.columns)"` 验证 endpoint 真实存在 + 数据形态**，再决定 schema。AC 是"目标语言"，数据源真实情况是"实现语言"，二者偏差是常态。
- **`stock_hot_rank_detail_em(symbol='SH600519'|'SZ000001')` 是 EastMoney 真正的 per-stock 历史人气 endpoint**：返回 365 日 (时间, 排名, 证券代码, 新晋粉丝, 铁杆粉丝)；symbol 要求带 EastMoney 风格的 SH/SZ 前缀（不是 .SH 后缀 — 与 Stock.symbol 形式不同）。新增了 `_infer_exchange_prefix` helper 从 6-digit 代码反推前缀，复用于本 story 同款 endpoint family（stock_hot_rank_relate_em / stock_hot_rank_latest_em / stock_hot_keyword_em 都用同样 symbol 形式）。
- **双重代理 (US-031/US-032 范式) 扩展到 data-layer**：之前 US-031 quality_high / US-032 earnings_surprise 把代理逻辑放在 factor 里；US-034 把代理逻辑**下沉到 sync 阶段写入物化列** — 因为 post_count = round(100000/rank) 是 per-row 算式 (不依赖横截面 / 时序状态)，Python 端一次算好让 TS 端 bulkCreate，因子层读 column 不读源。**优势**：(1) 未来引入真实数据只改 sync 一处，因子文件不动；(2) 用户 query StockSentiment.post_count 直接看到代理值，不会出现"模型字段不带代理含义"的 confusion。**判定**：若代理算式是 per-row 纯函数 → 落物化列；若代理需要跨期/跨股 aggregate → 留 factor 内。
- **API class name 沿用 AC 历史命名 + jsdoc 注解实际数据源**：`EastMoneyQAClient` 即便底层不是 股吧 Q&A endpoint。好处：(1) 跨 story 引用链稳定 (PRD / 测试 / 下游报告引用 `EastMoneyQAClient` 不变)；(2) AC vs 实际的 gap 通过 jsdoc 一句话补全。下次任何"AC endpoint 不存在 → 替代 endpoint"的 story 都按此处理。
- **Edit 同名函数易触发 Python 大坑**：写 Python helper 时无意中触发了 `_parse_date_cell` 函数重复 + 两个 `def main()` 错乱。Edit 的 `replace_all=false` 单点替换在长文件里很难 disambiguate。**下次大改 Python helper 用 Read + 手动确认行号范围 → 然后用唯一 anchor 字串做 Edit**，或直接 Write 整段子模块。
- **Scale-invariant ratio 因子的优势**：本因子 raw_value = recent/baseline 比率，对 post_count 的 absolute scale 完全不敏感 (×100000 / ×1000 缩放在 ratio 内相消)。这意味着未来真实 post_count 写入同一列（绝对单位 = 帖子数量），因子输出的数值范围 / zscore 分布完全不变，**完整保持 backtest / FactorIC 历史可比**。如果改成 absolute 量纲（如 "近 5 日 post_count 总和"），数据源切换后 zscore 失效，必须重跑整个历史。**ratio-family 因子在双重代理升级路径上天然鲁棒** — 下次代理类因子优先选 ratio 形式。
- **Pure-function-first factor design** (US-029/US-030/US-031/US-032/US-033 lineage continues): 14 个因子全部把 ALL non-DB logic 抽 `export function`s, `compute()` 只是 thin DB-query + assemble layer。让 77 个测试 1s 跑完不走 Sequelize。这个 lineage 是因子库的核心约定，新加因子 **不要把复杂数学塞 compute() 闭包里**，先抽 helper 再写 compute。
- **新增因子要 grep `names.length === <N>` 检查既有 factor count 断言**：本次 EarningsSurpriseFactor.test.ts 有硬编码 count=13，US-034 加因子后该 test 红了一个 assertion，需手动改 13→14。**未来 US-035 / US-036 加因子前 `grep "names.length === " backend/tests/factors/`** 一次，预先发现所有 count 类断言；最终更佳是把这类断言改成 ">= N" 或 "包含 X" — 但当前快速增量先保持单调更新模式。

## 2026/06/07 - US-035

### What was implemented
**15th factor + new data source** for the multi-factor library:
- **Sequelize model** `backend/src/models/ShareholderCount.ts` (composite PK `(report_date, stock_code)`) — fields: `holder_count` (AC required, INT > 0) + AKShare-derived `holder_count_prev/_change/_change_pct` (audit) + `interval_change_pct` + `avg_holder_market_cap` + `avg_holder_shares` + `total_market_cap/_shares` + **`share_change`** (送转股/增发数量，factor 层用来过滤股本变动期 holder_count 环比噪音) + `share_change_reason` + `announce_date` + `source` + `raw_payload` + 5 indexes.
- **Python helper** `get_shareholder_count(stock_code)` in `backend/python/akshare_helper.py` calling `stock_zh_a_gdhs_detail_em(symbol=<6-digit>)` — **PRD AC named `stock_zh_a_gdhs` but that endpoint only accepts `symbol='最新'` or `'YYYYMMDD'` (single snapshot for the whole market, no per-stock history)**. Substituted with `stock_zh_a_gdhs_detail_em` which returns full per-stock historical timeline (~50-70 quarterly snapshots / 10+ years). Robust col_map (4 sets of column synonyms), per-date dedup via `seen_dates: set`, `_parse_date_cell` reused.
- **`ShareholderCountClient`** (`backend/src/data/sources/`) mirroring AnalystForecastClient pattern (90s timeout via `SHAREHOLDER_COUNT_TIMEOUT_MS` env).
- **`ShareholderCountSyncService`** (`backend/src/data/services/`) with `syncStock` + `syncStocks` per-stock pattern (US-022/US-024/US-030 reused): bulkCreate + updateOnDuplicate idempotent reruns + 200ms friendly throttle + skip-existing checkpoint via `SHAREHOLDER_COUNT_SKIP_EXISTING` env. **No business derivation in service** — 最新 vs 上一期环比 是因子语义本身，留给 ShareholderConcentrationFactor 实时计算。
- **`ShareholderConcentrationFactor`** (`backend/src/quant/factors/library/`) — formula: `raw_value = -(holder_count[latest] - holder_count[prev]) / holder_count[prev]`. **取负** 让 holder_count 下降（集中）→ 正分；上升（分散）→ 负分. Category `flow`. Pure helpers (`computeConcentrationChange` + `ShareholderObservation` / `ConcentrationBreakdown` interfaces) all `export`ed for unit testing. Self-registers as `'shareholder_concentration'`.
- **CLI** `sync-shareholder-count.ts` + npm script `sync:shareholder-count` with `--stock` / `--stocks` / `--all` / `--listed-before` / `--interval-ms` / `--force` flags.
- **84-assertion unit test** `backend/tests/factors/ShareholderConcentrationFactor.test.ts` covering: 边界 / 退化 / 数据卫生 / lookahead bias guard / **share_change 过滤 5 边角** (default exclude=true vs exclude=false vs 上期变动但最新期无 vs share_change=null 视为 0 vs share_change 字段缺失) / 业务方向 / 多期排序 / 部分行 holder_count 缺失 / Factor metadata / Registry 集成 / 3 常量 / 15 因子全部存在 / 端到端机构吸筹+散户接盘场景 / 空 universe.
- **Stale assertion fixes**: bumped `EarningsSurpriseFactor.test.ts` (14→15) and `EastMoneyQAFactor.test.ts` (added `shareholder_concentration` to expectedFactors) to keep factor count assertions aligned with the new 15-factor registry.

### Learnings for future iterations
- **AC endpoint name reality-check** (extends US-034 lesson): again the AC named `stock_zh_a_gdhs` which doesn't have per-stock history mode. `stock_zh_a_gdhs_detail_em(symbol=<6-digit>)` is the actual endpoint. **Always `/usr/bin/python3 -c "import akshare as ak; help(ak.<fn_name>)"` verify the endpoint signature + try a sample call BEFORE designing the model schema.** AC is "target language", AKShare reality is "implementation language"; gap is normal.
- **"Business-condition exclusion" is a new factor guard category** (vs "data hygiene exclusion"): `share_change != 0` skip is the first guard where the data itself is computable but its business meaning is broken (送转股 / 增发 makes holder_count environmentally incomparable to prior quarter). Reusable for: ex-dividend close comparisons (PE before/after dividend), suspension-bridged price ratios, post-split fundamental ratios. **判据**：if data passes hygiene check but business interpretation requires "the world wasn't structurally disrupted", that's a business-condition guard — surface it as a default-on `EXCLUDE_<CONDITION>` boolean param so power users can override.
- **季度低频数据 → MIN_OBSERVATIONS_TOTAL=2 is correct, not too aggressive**: previous factors (analyst_consensus/east_money_qa) set 5-10 min observations because daily-frequency data has many points per window. ShareholderCount has ~4 obs/year quarterly, so requiring 2 (one current + one prev) is the minimum environmentally possible for env. **写新季度低频因子时不要照搬日级因子的 10+ 阈值** —— rethink based on data freq.
- **`stock_zh_a_gdhs_detail_em` returns "区间涨跌幅"** = prev-to-current 股价 % change between 上次截止日 → 本次截止日. Currently unused by `shareholder_concentration` but stored — future US-036+ "投资者结构 + 业绩驱动" 复合因子 (e.g. "高分红 + 户数下降 + 涨幅 < N%" = 低估值集中筹码) can use it without re-syncing.
- **API call substitution pattern formalized** (4-place documentation rule): when AC endpoint doesn't fit reality, the substitution rationale must appear in 4 places: Python helper docstring (the actual call) + TS Client jsdoc + Sync service jsdoc + Model column comment (where the substitution affects the schema). Factor jsdoc only needs to know about substitution when the substitute changes the column's semantics. This rule is now codified in `backend/src/data/CLAUDE.md`.
- **Composite PK + bulkCreate + updateOnDuplicate is idempotent for per-stock historical** (US-022/US-024/US-030/US-035 lineage): no need for in-memory dedup like AnalystForecast had to do — ShareholderCount PK is `(report_date, stock_code)` and same stock cannot legitimately have multiple snapshots on the same `report_date`. (Python helper has `seen_dates: set` as paranoid double-protection only.)
- **DECIMAL column type choice matters for `total_shares`**: started with `INTEGER` then switched to `BIGINT` — total_shares for large caps (贵州茅台 / 工商银行) exceeds 2^31-1 (~2.1B). Always use `BIGINT` for "股数" / "shares" columns. Caveat: Sequelize BIGINT becomes a string in some dialects; if anyone needs arithmetic they should `Number()` it explicitly (factor doesn't use this column so safe).
- **Factor count assertion update is now a checklist item**: 2 stale assertions in 2 test files needed updating. **`grep "names.length === " backend/tests/factors/`** before committing any new factor. (Mentioned in US-034 learning but kept hitting it; codifying here as the explicit pre-commit grep.)

## 2026/06/07 - US-036

### What was implemented
Added the **16th factor** `gradual_breakout` in `backend/src/quant/factors/library/GradualBreakoutFactor.ts` — a 价 + 量 配合累计因子capturing "渐进强爆" (温和放量逐步走强) signal per the AC formula:

  `raw_value = Σ_{近 30 个有效交易日} (daily_volume[i] / avg_60d_volume[i] - 1) × sign(close[i] - close[i-1])`

Constants: `RECENT_WINDOW_DAYS=30`, `VOLUME_BASELINE_DAYS=60`, `QUERY_CALENDAR_DAYS=150`, `MIN_VOLUME_BASELINE_OBS=30`, `MIN_RECENT_DAYS_FOR_VALID=21`.

**4 业务象限 (sign × ratio)**:
1. 价涨 + 量增 → **正贡献** (渐进建仓)
2. 价涨 + 量减 → 负贡献 (量价背离 / 拉高出货预警)
3. 价跌 + 量减 → **正贡献** (缩量调整不杀伤)
4. 价跌 + 量增 → 负贡献 (恐慌出货)

**Pure-function helpers all exported** (per the US-029+ factor test pattern): `extractSortedBars` / `computeChangeSigns` / `compute60dAvgVolumes` / `computeGradualBreakoutScore` + `SortedBar` / `BreakoutBreakdown` interfaces. Lets the 105-assertion test suite cover 4 business 象限 + sliding-baseline algorithm precision + tail-index alignment + data hygiene without touching the DB.

**Key design choices** (all documented in factor jsdoc):
- `change_pct` computed from `close[T]/close[T-1]-1` directly, **NOT read from `DailyBar.change_percent`** — that column is nullable and inconsistently backfilled across sync tasks; we already need `close` to compute the volume ratio anyway, so sign calculation is free.
- `sign()` is three-state `{-1, 0, +1}` not `Math.sign()` (latter would technically work since both produce 3 states at 0 boundary, but explicit branching makes the intent — and the "数据异常 close ≤ 0 → sign=0" defensive fallback — clearer at the call site).
- `baseline` is 60 prior days **excluding the current day** (`base[i] = mean(volume[i-60..i-1])`), the standard practice — including current day in baseline would bias the ratio.
- tail-index alignment: take last 30 bars from `bars[length-30..length-1]` clamped to `bars[length-baselineDays..length-1]` to ensure each `i` has a valid baseline; identical to `momentum` / `momentum_reversal` (US-010 / US-033) tail-index pattern; absorbs holiday gaps naturally without calendar arithmetic.
- 3 失效 guards 协同: bars 不足 `VOLUME_BASELINE_DAYS+1=61` → skip; 60 日有效 volume < 30 → baseline null; 30 日内有效贡献天 < 21 (= 30×70%) → return null (stop loss for 停牌过多 stocks).

Factor self-registers to `factorRegistry` as `name='gradual_breakout'`, `category='momentum'`. Library `index.ts` imports it alphabetically (between `EastMoneyQAFactor` and `GrowthFactor`).

### Files changed
- `backend/src/quant/factors/library/GradualBreakoutFactor.ts` (new, ~270 LOC including docstring + pure helpers + Factor body + register)
- `backend/src/quant/factors/library/index.ts` (added import + updated top-of-file factor count comment)
- `backend/src/quant/factors/CLAUDE.md` (title 15→16 factors; segment lede; factor reference table row; z-score-friendly bullet list entry)
- `backend/tests/factors/GradualBreakoutFactor.test.ts` (new, 105 assertions covering constants + 3 pure helpers + 4 business 象限 + 数据卫生 + metadata + 16-factor count assertion + 空 universe path)
- `backend/tests/factors/EastMoneyQAFactor.test.ts` (synced 15→16: added 'gradual_breakout' to expectedFactors array)
- `backend/tests/factors/ShareholderConcentrationFactor.test.ts` (synced 15→16: same)
- `backend/tests/factors/EarningsSurpriseFactor.test.ts` (synced 15→16: count comment + assertion)
- `ralph/prd.json` (US-036 → passes:true + detailed notes)

- `cd backend && ./node_modules/.bin/eslint src/quant/factors/library/GradualBreakoutFactor.ts src/quant/factors/library/index.ts --quiet --fix` → exit 0 (1 prettier auto-fix to GradualBreakoutFactor.ts)
- `cd backend && npx ts-node --transpile-only tests/factors/GradualBreakoutFactor.test.ts` → **105 passed, 0 failed** < 1s

  - AnalystConsensus 54 / EarningsSurprise 77 / EastMoneyQA 78 / Liquidity 53 / MomentumReversal 74 / QualityHigh 54 / ShareholderConcentration 85 = **475 ok / 0 failed**

### Browser verification
**Not applicable** — backend-only story (factor library addition, no UI). The factor will auto-appear in `/workspace/factors` when listed via `GET /api/factors/overview` (US-015 reads the registry).

### Learnings for future iterations
- **Sliding baseline ≠ static baseline**: my first test cut of "场景 2 价涨量减 score = -5" was wrong because `avg_60d_volume[i]` is a sliding window — as low-volume recent days slide into the past, the baseline at later `i` is lower, making the ratio less negative. **The test must compute expected values dynamically with a loop matching the sliding algorithm**: `for k=0..9, baseline_k = (high_vol*(60-k) + low_vol*k)/60`. The factor is correct; the test's mental model wasn't. Hand-calculated constants are fine for static baselines (e.g. always-2000); for true sliding windows, write a tiny reference impl inside the test that exercises the same arithmetic the factor does. (Catches errors in **both** sides — if both match, you've verified the algorithm conceptually rather than memorized a constant.)
- **`change_percent` column avoidance is reusable**: `DailyBar.change_percent` is nullable + inconsistently backfilled across sync tasks (per US-005..US-008 lessons). When a factor needs sign(daily change), compute it from close[T]/close[T-1]-1 in memory — we already pulled `close` for any momentum/volume factor, and the in-loop arithmetic cost is zero. Don't depend on `change_percent` unless the factor truly only needs the percentage value (not just the sign) and the data source for this factor is guaranteed to have it populated. (Documented in factor jsdoc; codify here.)
- **价+量配合因子 vs 纯价格因子 vs 纯成交量因子 — 三类不冗余**: `momentum` / `momentum_reversal` are pure-price (sign of return); `liquidity` is pure-volume (turnover rate magnitude); `gradual_breakout` is the price-volume composite. Same raw close+volume data but the three formulas extract different alpha: pure-momentum says "is it trending"; pure-liquidity says "is it liquid"; gradual_breakout says "do price and volume agree". Estimated correlation < 0.5 (FactorIC US-041 will verify). When a new factor reads only `close + volume`, ask first whether one of these three covers its hypothesis before defaulting to "let's combine these differently".
- **Factor count assertion pre-commit grep**: Already a documented pattern (US-034 learning) but worth restating — `grep -n "names.length === \|expectedFactors\.length === \|共 15 个" backend/tests/factors/` (substitute the previous count) catches stale `===` constants. US-036 hit 3 files needing sync; US-035 hit 2. The number keeps growing because every new factor is bumped.

## 2026/06/07 - US-037

### What was implemented
**GridSearchOptimizer — 多维参数网格调优引擎** at `backend/src/quant/backtest/GridSearchOptimizer.ts`. Public class with `optimize(input, options?)` entrypoint that takes a strategy_key + ParamGrid (`{topN: [10,20,30,50], stopLossPct: [-5,-7,-10]}`) + base backtest config, generates the cartesian product, runs N backtests (default via `quantBacktestEngine.run()`; injectable `BacktestRunner` for unit tests), computes a multi-objective `composite_score = sharpe * w_sharpe + annual_return * w_annual - max_drawdown * w_drawdown` (default weights 1.0 / 0.4 / 0.5 — sharpe-dominant), ranks results, and persists to 2 new DB tables (`optimization_runs` + `optimization_results`). CLI script `npm run run:grid-search` exposes all the knobs + 3 admin modes (`--list / --show / --cleanup-days`).

### Files changed
- **New model `backend/src/models/OptimizationRun.ts`** — single auto-increment PK `id`, columns: strategy_name / param_grid_json / backtest_config_json / status (pending|running|completed|failed) / total_combos / completed_combos / failed_combos / best_result_id (FK back-pointer for "最优参数" 查询) / created_by / error_message / started_at / finished_at + timestamps. 4 indexes (strategy_name / status / created_by / created_at).
- **New model `backend/src/models/OptimizationResult.ts`** — single auto-increment PK `id`, columns: run_id (FK) / combo_index (0-based ordinal per run) / params_json / sharpe / annual_return / max_drawdown / total_return / win_rate / trade_count (all nullable for failed combos) / composite_score (nullable) / status / error_message / duration_seconds + timestamps. 3 indexes (run_id / run_id+composite_score / run_id+status).
- **Registered both models in `backend/src/models/index.ts` + `backend/src/config/database.ts`** so `sequelize.sync({alter:true})` auto-creates tables in dev mode.
- **New `backend/src/quant/backtest/GridSearchOptimizer.ts` (~530 LOC)** — single class file with:
  - 3 export纯函数 (`generateGrid` cartesian product / `computeCompositeScore` multi-objective formula / `sortByCompositeScoreDesc` ranking) — independently unit-testable, no Sequelize dependency.
  - `OptimizationResultRecord` plain-object interface (mirrors the Sequelize model fields 1-to-1 but no Model methods) — this is the **public return type** of optimize() in both persist=true and persist=false modes. Lets the test suite construct mock records without booting Sequelize.
  - `BacktestRunner` injectable function-type `(combo, options) => Promise<BacktestSummary>` — default `defaultBacktestRunner` lazy-imports quantBacktestEngine + quantDataService (so unit tests don't pay that cost); tests inject `runner: async ({params}) => ({sharpe: ..., annual_return: ..., max_drawdown: ...})`.
  - `runWithConcurrency` helper for chunked batching (concurrency > 1 uses Promise.all per chunk, batches serial; concurrency ≤ 1 strictly sequential). No Bull dependency.
  - Per-combo try/catch failure isolation: 1 combo error → that row gets `status='failed' + error_message`, other combos unaffected.
  - `optimize()` writes OptimizationRun first (status='running'), runs combos, then UPDATE to status='completed' + best_result_id回写. persist=false skips all DB.
  - 4 admin methods: `getRunResults(run_id)` / `listRuns(opts)` / `deleteRun(run_id)` / `cleanupOlderThan(days)`.
- **New CLI `backend/src/scripts/run-grid-search.ts`** with commander + 4 modes:
  - optimize (default): --strategy / --start / --end / --grid / --capital / --benchmark / --universe / --symbols / --max-combos / --concurrency / --w-* (weights) / --no-persist / --user-id
  - admin: --list / --show=<id> / --cleanup-days=<n>
  - Exit codes 0 / 1 / 2 (success / soft-fail / hard-fail).
- **`backend/package.json` npm script `run:grid-search`**.
- **New `backend/tests/backtest/grid-search-optimizer.test.ts`** — 100 ok / 0 fail < 200ms — covering generateGrid (8 cases), computeCompositeScore (15), sortByCompositeScoreDesc (9), and 16 async end-to-end optimize() scenarios (happy path / failure isolation / all-fail / max_combos cap / custom weights / injected runner skips registry / empty grid {} / empty dim throws / concurrency=3 / runner-receives-correct-options / duration_seconds recorded / unregistered strategy throws / in-memory all fields / negative max_drawdown normalized / ranked-best consistent / single combo). Uses **named-async-function + main() awaits sequentially** pattern (NOT the IIFE pattern used by 30+ prior tests — see learning below).
- **Updated `backend/src/quant/CLAUDE.md`** with a new "US-037: GridSearchOptimizer — 参数网格调优" section: design constraints + relationship with existing `QuantBacktestService.createParameterGridBacktests` (Bull-queue path vs in-process) + extension guide for US-038 BayesianOptimizer / US-039 WalkForwardValidator.

### Quality checks
- `cd backend && npx tsc --noEmit` → exit 0 (clean)
- `cd backend && ./node_modules/.bin/eslint src/quant/backtest/GridSearchOptimizer.ts src/models/OptimizationRun.ts src/models/OptimizationResult.ts src/models/index.ts src/config/database.ts src/scripts/run-grid-search.ts --quiet --fix` → exit 0 (8 prettier auto-fixed across 3 files)

### Browser verification
**Not applicable** — backend-only story (new optimizer + 2 DB tables + CLI). UI consumption will land in US-016 策略实验室 "参数调优" tab.

### Learnings for future iterations

- **`OptimizationResult.build()` requires Sequelize initialized** — my first cut of the test file used `OptimizationResult.build(record)` to construct in-memory rows for sort assertions. This **crashed** at runtime with `ModelNotInitializedError: Model not initialized: Member "build" cannot be called` because the test doesn't bootstrap `sequelize` (we want to stay DB-free). **Fix**: introduce a plain-object interface `OptimizationResultRecord` that mirrors the model 1-to-1, and have `optimize(persist=false)` return that directly. `optimize(persist=true)` ALSO returns the same plain shape (just sets `id` from the persisted model). `getRunResults()` does the inverse: DB → plain via `modelToRecord()`. Net result: **the return type is a plain object in both modes**, sort/best/ranked logic is identical, the test doesn't need Sequelize, and the caller has a stable type signature.
- **`(async function name(){})()` IIFE pattern does NOT block subsequent code** — my first test file had 16 IIFEs in the optimize() block + a `testFinalSummary` IIFE at the end. The IIFEs are fire-and-forget Promises; `testFinalSummary` would print the summary _before_ the other async tests finished (96 ok shown but `concurrency=3` and `duration_seconds` tests silently skipped). **Fix**: convert each test to a **named async function** (no IIFE), then a `main()` awaits them in sequence + the summary at the end. This is now the canonical pattern for async test scripts in `backend/tests/**`. The existing strategy tests (e.g. MultiFactorAlphaStrategy.test.ts) work as IIFEs because they're synchronous-only; any test with `async function` blocks must use the named-await pattern.
- **Plain object record vs Sequelize Model — the return-type unification trick** — when an entity is sometimes persisted, sometimes not, sometimes round-tripped through DB, a plain interface type is _much_ easier to work with than `OptimizationResult | OptimizationResultDataValues | {...partial}` unions. The factory functions (model-to-plain via `modelToRecord`, raw-to-plain inline in `optimize()`) keep the conversion focused and explicit. Repeat the pattern for future US-038 / US-039 / US-040 outputs (`WalkForwardResult` / `RegimeBacktestResult`).
- **`base_config: Omit<QuantBacktestOptions, 'strategy_keys' | 'params_by_strategy'>`** is the cleanest input shape. Caller doesn't pass these two fields; optimize() composes them per combo. Saves boilerplate at every callsite and prevents the bug "caller pre-fills `strategy_keys`, optimizer adds it again, gets a duplicate".
- **Lazy-import `quantBacktestEngine + quantDataService` inside `defaultBacktestRunner`** — `GridSearchOptimizer.ts` is imported by the CLI + future US-016 controller + tests. If you `import { quantBacktestEngine } from './internal/QuantBacktestEngine'` at the top, you pay the cost of pulling the entire quant subsystem (160 file deep tree) even when the consumer is using a fake runner. `require()` inside the runner defers that cost until production use. Future "optional engine" runners should follow the same pattern.
- **strategy_key validation only when no runner injected** — gate the `strategyRegistry.get(strategy_key)` check behind `!options.runner`. With a fake runner, the test's strategy_key is a label (e.g. `'fake_strategy_for_test'`) that doesn't need to exist anywhere. Avoids forcing test code to either (a) register a fake strategy in the live registry (state pollution) or (b) Symbol-patch the registry. Documented as a US-037 design constraint in `quant/CLAUDE.md`.
- **`new OptimizationRun` vs `new OptimizationResult` — auto-increment PKs require a fresh `create()`** — both models use `autoIncrement: true` on `id`, so `.create()` (not `.build().save()`) is the correct constructor for getting a populated `id` field back. `create()` does an INSERT + reads RETURNING id (Postgres) or LAST_INSERT_ID (MySQL) atomically. After `optimize()` resolves, all results have their persisted `id` and the run has its `best_result_id` written.
- **`Object.freeze(DEFAULT_COMPOSITE_WEIGHTS)`** — small but important: this is a default-value constant; freezing prevents accidental `DEFAULT_COMPOSITE_WEIGHTS.sharpe = 2.0` mutation that would silently affect all subsequent runs. Repeat for any module-level "default config" constant.
- **`max_combos` safety cap default = 256** — a 5-维 grid with 6 values each = 7,776 combos. Without a cap, a user typo would blow up DB / runtime. 256 is reasonable for most use cases; users can override up to 4096 (still bounded). Documented in jsdoc + CLI help.
- **`OptimizationRun.best_result_id` is nullable** — set after the run completes. NULL while running, NULL if all combos failed (best=null). Index-friendly: the `(run_id, composite_score)` composite index on `OptimizationResult` makes "rank within run" queries fast regardless of `best_result_id` write order.
- **Two grid-search systems coexist (US-014 createParameterGridBacktests vs US-037 GridSearchOptimizer)** — intentionally. Old one persists per-combo as `QuantBacktestTask` rows + Bull queue (suits UI background jobs); new one persists as `OptimizationRun + OptimizationResult` rows + in-process sync (suits CLI / embedded walk-forward / Bayesian baseline). **DON'T merge them**: they answer different ops needs and have different latency / persistence shapes. Documented in `quant/CLAUDE.md` "与 createParameterGridBacktests 的关系" subsection.

### Codebase Patterns added (to consolidated section at top)
- `OptimizationResultRecord` plain-object pattern → recommend for US-038/039/040
- IIFE async pattern can drop tests → use `main() { await ... }` for any test with async blocks
- Lazy-import inside default runner → for any "default impl pulls big subsystem" pattern
- `Omit<Options, 'X' | 'Y'>` for "caller doesn't touch these, optimizer composes per combo"
- strategy_key validation gated by `!options.runner` for test-friendliness

## 2026/06/07 - US-038

### What was implemented
**贝叶斯（高斯过程 + EI）参数搜索优化器** — US-037 GridSearchOptimizer 的姐妹模块，专为连续 / 整数参数空间的高效搜索设计。

**核心架构选择**：自实现 EI + RBF GP 而非引入 npm 包：
- `bayesian-optimization` 包近 4 年未更新且依赖 ml-matrix（多 MB）
- EI + RBF GP 的核心数学不超过 200 行，自实现可保持纯函数 + 可独立单测
- 同 seed → 完全相同的采样序列，测试 / 回测复现性 100%

**端到端管道**：
1. **Schema** — `OptimizationRun` 加 `optimizer_type` STRING(20) defaultValue='grid_search' 字段区分两种优化器；`param_grid_json` 类型 `Record<string, any[]>` → `Record<string, any>` 让 JSONB 列承载 grid 网格 (数组) 和 bayesian bounds (对象) 两种 shape
2. **核心模块** `backend/src/quant/backtest/BayesianOptimizer.ts` (~750 LOC) — 8 个 export 纯函数 + `BayesianOptimizer` class with `optimize()` 入口 + 4 个 admin 方法 (getRunResults / listRuns / deleteRun / cleanupOlderThan)，全部 import 自 GridSearchOptimizer 的共享 API (BacktestRunner / OptimizationResultRecord / CompositeScoreWeights / computeCompositeScore / sortByCompositeScoreDesc / defaultBacktestRunner)
3. **CLI** `backend/src/scripts/run-bayesian-opt.ts` (~270 LOC) + `npm run run:bayesian-opt` 支持 4 模式 (optimize / --list / --show / --cleanup-days)
4. **Tests** `backend/tests/backtest/bayesian-optimizer.test.ts` (~830 LOC) **161 ok / 0 failed in ~830ms**

### Files changed
- **New**: `backend/src/quant/backtest/BayesianOptimizer.ts` (核心模块)
- **New**: `backend/src/scripts/run-bayesian-opt.ts` (CLI)
- **New**: `backend/tests/backtest/bayesian-optimizer.test.ts` (161 测试)
- **Edit**: `backend/src/models/OptimizationRun.ts` (加 optimizer_type 字段 + 调整 param_grid_json 类型)
- **Edit**: `backend/package.json` (加 run:bayesian-opt npm script)
- **Edit**: `backend/src/quant/CLAUDE.md` (新增 US-038 一节：对比表 + 6 设计约束 + 扩展指南 + 测试模式)
- **Edit**: `ralph/prd.json` (US-038 → passes:true)
- **Edit**: `ralph/progress.txt` (本条目 + 新增 Codebase Patterns)

### Learnings for future iterations

**核心技术学习 — 数学算法的纯函数化**：
- 自实现的数学算法（EI / RBF kernel / Cholesky / GP posterior）全部 `export function`，让单测可在 ms 级跑完 161 用例不走 DB。回想 US-029 LiquidityFactor 的 quantileAtSortedAsc + sampleStddev 抽出 export 模式，这次扩到 8 个 helper —— 复杂数学算法的 helper 不能塞进 class private method，否则单测无法精确断言（手算 Cholesky [[4,2],[2,3]] → [[2,0],[1,√2]] 这种关键回归测试只能 export 后才能写）。
- **手算回归测试是数学正确性的金标准**：把已知公式的精确解写成 expectClose 测试（如 normalCDF(1.96)≈0.975 / Cholesky [[4,2],[2,3]] / EI std=0→0）。下次改 GP 实现时，回归测试就是保险栓。

**复现性原则 — SeededRandom 替代 Math.random**：
- US-038 deliberately **never** introduces Math.random into optimizer code。同 seed + 同 bounds + 同 runner → 100% identical 采样序列。这让：
  - 单测可以断言"第 7 个采样点 x = 7.234"这种精确值，不需要 fuzzy "x ≈ 7"
  - 回测可复算论文结果（人间无 Math.random）
  - 失败追踪：用户报 "种子 999 跑出来奇怪结果"，开发者跑同 seed 立刻复现
- Park-Miller LCG 周期 2^31-1 对贝叶斯优化 ~100 次采样完全够；不要为了"更高质量随机"换 Mersenne Twister （引入复杂度无收益）。
- **下次任何需要"随机但可复现"的算法（蒙特卡洛 US-043 / 多策略组合采样 US-044 / 因子 walk-forward US-039）都用 SeededRandom**。

**复用既有 API 而非重新实现**：
- BayesianOptimizer **没有** 重新定义 BacktestRunner / OptimizationResultRecord / CompositeScoreWeights / computeCompositeScore / sortByCompositeScoreDesc / defaultBacktestRunner —— 全部 import 自 GridSearchOptimizer。理由：
  - 让 caller 用同一段代码消费两种优化器输出（CLI 报告 / UI 表格 / WalkForward 嵌套）
  - 让 sort + score 公式有单一事实源，加新维度（如 sortino）只在 GridSearchOptimizer 一处加
  - 减少 750 LOC → ~600 LOC（节省 150 LOC pure code reuse）
- **判据**：当新模块在概念上是"同类操作不同算法"时（两种 optimizer / 两种 sampler / 两种 scoring）— 拒绝平行写第二份 type / function，全部 import 共享。

**Sequelize alter 兼容范式**：
- 给已有表加新列（OptimizationRun.optimizer_type）必须有 `defaultValue='grid_search'` 让旧行迁移后自动归类，不需 backfill。新插行 (bayesian) 走 `optimizer_type: 'bayesian'`。`listRuns({ include_all_types: true })` 让"我所有调优历史"视图能跨两种优化器。
- 改既有列类型（JSONB Record<string, any[]> → Record<string, any>）是**类型扩窄而非扩宽**，向下兼容（之前的数组值是 any 的子集）。下次类似改动先确认是 extends 不是 narrows。

**GP 数学的"失败点不进训练集"约束**：
- NaN 进入 K 矩阵会让 Cholesky 抛"非正定"错；让一次 sync 失败的 iter 污染后续所有 EI 选点是灾难。所以 BayesianOptimizer 显式：(1) try/catch per iter; (2) 失败时 status='failed' + error_message 写入 results 数组但 (3) 不 push 到 observations。下次任何"数据驱动的 acquisition function"（Thompson sampling / GP-UCB / NSGA-II crossover）都要复用此 guard。

**EI candidate 网格的维度退化**：
- 用户传 5 维 bounds 时全 cartesian (gridSize^5 = 64^5 ≈ 10^9 内存爆炸)。BayesianOptimizer 在 D ≥ 4 时退化为 random sampling min(50_000, gridSize × D × 4)。**新算法引入时务必显式做维度复杂度分析**，写在 jsdoc 顶部和 CLAUDE.md，让用户知道"我能跑多少维"。

### Codebase Patterns added (to consolidated section at top)
- **共享 model 表 + 新 discriminator 字段范式** (US-037 + US-038): 当两个相关模块（grid vs bayesian / 同步 vs 异步 / regime A vs regime B）应该共享同一组 DB 表时，加一个 STRING(20) discriminator 字段（如 `optimizer_type`）+ defaultValue 让旧行自动归类，避免新增平行表。listXxx() 默认 filter by discriminator，但提供 `include_all_types=true` 让"全部历史"视图能跨类型。Sequelize alter 自动加列零迁移成本。
- **SeededRandom 替代 Math.random**: 所有需要"随机但可复现"的算法（贝叶斯优化 / 蒙特卡洛 / Walk-forward 子集采样 / 多策略权重扰动）都用 `SeededRandom` (Park-Miller LCG)。**永不引入 `Math.random`** 到任何 backtest / optimizer / 评估代码——会让测试 / 论文复现 / 用户报错追踪都失败。
- **数学算法 export function 全开放**: 像 BayesianOptimizer 的 normalCDF / rbfKernel / choleskyDecompose / gaussianProcessPosterior / expectedImprovement / pickNextByEI 这些数学 helper 全部 `export function`，让单测能在 ms 级跑过 100+ 数学边界。**手算关键回归测试是金标准**：写 expectClose(normalCDF(1.96), 0.975) / Cholesky [[4,2],[2,3]] → [[2,0],[1,√2]] / EI std=0 → 0 这种已知答案，下次改实现就有保险栓。
- **失败点不进训练集 (US-038)**: GP / Bayesian / 其他"数据驱动的 acquisition function" 模块必须 try/catch per iter + 失败时记 status='failed' 但不 push 到 GP observations。NaN 进入 K 矩阵会让 Cholesky 抛"非正定"错，污染整轮采样。
- **维度复杂度退化策略 (US-038)**: D ≤ 3 cartesian (gridSize^D); D ≥ 4 退化为随机采样 min(N_max, gridSize × D × 4)。任何"对每维做全展开"的算法都要在 jsdoc 顶部 + CLAUDE.md 写显式维度上限，让用户知道何时切退化路径。
- **复用既有 API 而非平行写**: 第二种"同类操作不同算法"模块（如 US-038 第二种 optimizer）必须 import 共享 API (BacktestRunner / OptimizationResultRecord / sortByCompositeScoreDesc / computeCompositeScore) 而非平行定义 type / function。判据：新模块在概念上是"换算法保接口"时拒绝重复实现，保单一事实源。
---

## 2026/06/07 - US-039

### What was implemented
Built the **WalkForwardValidator** — the third member of the parameter-optimization triad (after US-037 GridSearchOptimizer and US-038 BayesianOptimizer). Rolling train → embedded grid search → test → roll forward → aggregate. The core anti-overfit detector: a strategy that's in-sample sharpe 1.8 but out-of-sample 0.3 gets caught here.

### Files changed
- `backend/src/models/WalkForwardResult.ts` (new, 229 lines) — Sequelize model with all train/test metrics + status + train_run_id FK
- `backend/src/quant/backtest/WalkForwardValidator.ts` (new, ~840 lines) — pure helpers (isoDateAddDays/isoDateAddMonths/compareIsoDate/sampleStddev/generateWalkForwardWindows/aggregateWindowMetrics) + WalkForwardValidator class with validate() + 4 admin methods
- `backend/src/scripts/run-walk-forward.ts` (new, ~290 lines) — CLI with optimize / --list / --show / --cleanup-days modes
- `backend/src/models/index.ts` + `backend/src/config/database.ts` — register WalkForwardResult
- `backend/package.json` — add `run:walk-forward` script
- `backend/tests/backtest/walk-forward-validator.test.ts` (new, ~1000 lines) — 141 unit tests covering 6 pure helpers + 17 e2e scenarios; uses fake optimizer + fake testRunner double-injection to stay DB-free
- `backend/src/quant/CLAUDE.md` — append 'US-039: WalkForwardValidator' section with three-way comparison table (GridSearch / Bayesian / WalkForward), 9 design constraints, expansion guidance, test patterns

### Quality checks
- `cd backend && npx tsc --noEmit` → exit 0
- `eslint src/models/WalkForwardResult.ts src/quant/backtest/WalkForwardValidator.ts src/scripts/run-walk-forward.ts src/models/index.ts src/config/database.ts --quiet --fix` → exit 0 (13 prettier auto-fixed)
- `npx ts-node tests/backtest/walk-forward-validator.test.ts` → 141 pass / 0 fail in <1s
- Regression: grid-search-optimizer (100 ok) / bayesian-optimizer (161 ok) / ashare-constraints (64 ok) / MultiFactorAlphaStrategy / EnsembleStrategy / EarningsSurpriseFactor → all still pass
- CLI `npm run run:walk-forward -- --help` boots cleanly

### Learnings for future iterations
- **Walk-forward = test_months 是滚动步长不是 train_months**: train 窗口可以重叠（相邻窗口共享 train 数据没关系），但 test 窗口必须完全不重叠（否则样本外信号重复污染）。所以 step = testMonths，不是 trainMonths。这点在 jsdoc 顶部明确强调，且测试用例 `w${i} test_start > w${i - 1} test_end` 是回归基线。
- **isoDateAddMonths 闰年月末 clamp** (1-31 → 闰年 2-29 / 平年 2-28) 是经典 off-by-one 大坑——任何月度滚动算法都必须处理。用 `Date.UTC(year, month, 0)` 算 daysInMonth 是最简单 robust 的写法（month 是 1-indexed，传 0 day 返回上月最后一天）。手算 10 个边界测试是回归保险栓。
- **共享 OptimizationRun 表 + 多 optimizer_type 的两年级嵌套**: walk-forward 的 父 run.optimizer_type='walk_forward'，**但**每个 train 窗口又通过嵌入式 GridSearchOptimizer 创建一个 *子* OptimizationRun.optimizer_type='grid_search'。两级嵌套是同表 + 不同 type，通过 `WalkForwardResult.train_run_id` 关联。`deleteRun` / `cleanupOlderThan` 必须递归清理子 runs，否则孤儿数据。
- **best_result_id 字段在父 run 上的语义因 optimizer_type 而不同**: GridSearch/Bayesian 父 run.best_result_id 指向 OptimizationResult.id（最好的 combo）；WalkForward 父 run.best_result_id 指向 WalkForwardResult.id（最好的窗口）。**消费方必须按 optimizer_type 分支处理**，写到了 jsdoc + CLAUDE.md。未来 US-016 UI 跨 type 列出 best 时要先 switch。
- **错误隔离 per-window 模式**: 与 strategies/ 的 per-block error fallback 同款。train_failed / test_failed 是两个独立错误状态——train 全 combo 失败 vs test backtest 抛错——分别记录让 ops 一眼看出窗口是因数据不足（test 缺数据）还是参数空间不当（train 全失败）。
- **`aggregateWindowMetrics` 的 out_of_sample_decay 是 walk-forward 最有诊断价值的输出**：mean(train_sharpe - test_sharpe)。>0 表示过拟合（in-sample 高 out-of-sample 低），>1 是严重，>2 是灾难。**做 walk-forward 的目的就是看这个指标**，不是看 mean_test_sharpe，否则不如直接跑 GridSearch。
- **DataSource 双注入（optimizer + testRunner）让单测毫秒级运行**: 测试可同时注入 fake EmbeddedOptimizer (返回固定 best_params) + fake BacktestRunner (返回固定 test_sharpe)，141 个测试 < 1s。`makeFakeOptimizer(pickByStart)` 让不同 train_start 返回不同 params + sharpe，模拟真实 walk-forward 在不同时段选不同 params 的行为；`makeFakeTestRunner(bySharpe)` 同样按 (params, startDate) 分发，让 best_window / win_ratio 等聚合断言可预测。
- **CLI npm script naming follows `run:` prefix convention** (与 run:grid-search / run:bayesian-opt 同款)。新加任何"长时间任务"CLI 都用此前缀。`sync:` 前缀留给数据同步类。
- **Test count assertions 数字 hardcoded 而非动态算 — 必须用 explicit 算式注释**: 几个 windows count 测试一开始 wrong，因为我没在脑子里精确算 cartesian 而是猜的。修复后每个数字旁边都加了 explicit 注释（"24m / train=12 test=3 → 4 windows" + 详细的 w0..w3 train_start/test_start 列表）。下次写滚动测试先在注释里画时间轴再写数字。

### Codebase Patterns added (consolidated to top Codebase Patterns section)
- 嵌入式优化器复用模式 (US-039 EmbeddedOptimizer interface)
- 滚动窗口算法的 testMonths-as-stride 不变量
- discriminator-keyed parent table + N-level nesting (递归 cleanup 模式)
- best_result_id 跨 optimizer_type 语义差异
- isoDateAddMonths 月末 clamp 标准实现
- out_of_sample_decay 是 walk-forward 唯一价值输出
---

## 2026/06/07 - US-040

### What was implemented

US-040 — **回测体系：分段市场环境回测报告**：拿一次已完成回测的 equity_curve +
trades，按市场环境（bull / bear / range / volatile）切成 N 个连续段，对每段独
立算 收益 / 夏普 / 最大回撤 / 胜率 / 成交数。回答"这个策略到底在哪种行情下赚
钱、在哪种行情下亏钱"这个 walk-forward 看不到的视角。

**End-to-end pipeline**:

1. **Sequelize 模型** `backend/src/models/RegimeBacktestResult.ts` — 字段：
   id / **run_id** (引用 QuantBacktestResult.id) / segment_index / strategy_key /
   benchmark_symbol / regime / start_date / end_date / day_count /
   return_pct / sharpe / drawdown_pct / win_rate / trade_count /
   equity_start / equity_end + 6 个 indexes (run_id, run_id+segment_index
   unique, run_id+regime, strategy_key+regime, regime, start_date)。**strategy_key
   + benchmark_symbol 物化** 让"multi_factor_alpha 在 bull regime 历次回测
   的平均表现"跨 run 聚合查询不必 JOIN 父表。Registered in models/index.ts
   + config/database.ts。
2. **核心模块** `backend/src/quant/backtest/RegimeSegmentedBacktest.ts`
   (~570 LOC) — `RegimeSegmentedBacktest` class with `segment(input, options?)`
   主入口 + 4 个 admin (`getRunSegments` / `deleteRun` / `cleanupOlderThan`
   / `listRecentRuns`)；**7 个 export 纯函数** (mapRawRegimeToSegmentRegime
   / mergeAdjacentSegments / sampleStddev / mean / maxDrawdownPctFromEquity
   / computeSegmentMetrics / aggregateRegimeSegments) 独立单测。
3. **RegimeSource 接口注入** (与 WalkForward.EmbeddedOptimizer 对齐): 生产
   `PRODUCTION_REGIME_SOURCE` 走 `marketEnvironmentService.getEnvironmentForStock(
   benchmark, {as_of: <date>})` per-day 采样自带 try/catch 兜底 'range'；
   测试注入 `makeFakeRegimeSource(stamps: Record<date, regime>)` 完全脱离
   MarketEnvironmentService + DB。
4. **CLI** `backend/src/scripts/run-regime-backtest.ts` (~200 LOC) 支持 5 模式：
   主流程 `--backtest-result-id=<n>` (with `--benchmark` / `--no-persist` /
   `--no-replace`) + 4 个 admin (`--list` / `--show=<n>` / `--delete-run=<n>`
   / `--cleanup-days=<n>`)；退出码 0=成功 / 2=严重错误。npm script
   `run:regime-backtest`。

**核心算法**：equity_curve 每个 date → resolveRegime → DailyRegimeStamp →
`mergeAdjacentSegments` (run-length encode) → per-segment equity slice +
trades slice → `computeSegmentMetrics` → `aggregateRegimeSegments`。

**关键设计决策**:

1. **不复用 OptimizationRun 父表** — 与 US-037/US-038/US-039 不同，US-040 是
   "对一次已完成 backtest 做事后分析"，不是优化任务，直接通过 run_id 引用
   QuantBacktestResult.id 即可。引入第 4 张 run 表既无必要也违背"只有真正
   不同 schema 才新表"原则。
2. **4 种 regime 是单一事实源** — 与 EnsembleStrategy.EnsembleMarketRegime
   完全一致 (bull/bear/range/volatile)；`mapRawRegimeToSegmentRegime` 独立
   实现 (不 import 自 strategies/) 避免 quant/backtest → quant/strategies
   反向依赖，但 jsdoc 明示语义 100% 镜像。
3. **trade 关联以 sell_date 为准** — 段内成交统计反映"该段实际兑现的盈亏"。
   入场跨段、出场在该段的 trade 也算入该段；未平仓 trade (sell_date 未定义)
   不计入任何段。
4. **sharpe 不足 5 个日收益时为 null** — 段太短无统计意义，写 null 让下游
   aggregate avg_sharpe 自动过滤。
5. **drawdown_pct 永远是正数** (与 WalkForwardResult / QuantBacktestResult
   同口径，跨表 SUM/MAX 无需 ABS())。
6. **`replace_existing=true` 默认覆盖式重算** — 同 run_id 已有 segments 时
   先 destroy 避免历史段+新段混在一起；CLI `--no-replace` 关闭。
7. **持久化 vs in-memory 模式并存** — `equity_curve+trades` 直接传 in-memory
   跳过 DB round-trip (嵌入式调用/单测)，`quant_backtest_result_id` 走 DB
   (CLI/UI)，同一 `segment()` 入口两种入参形态。
8. **per-day 串行 await** (非 Promise.all) — 避免 MarketEnvironmentService
   并发查 DB 撞 cache，且回测期间通常 200-500 个交易日单次跑可控。
9. **`maxDrawdownPctFromEquity` 独立实现** (不 import QuantMath) — 避免
   quant/backtest → quant/engine 反向依赖；返回百分数 (非小数)，与
   RegimeBacktestResult 列单位一致。
10. **strategy_key 缺失时退化为 'unknown' + warning** — 让 in-memory 模式
    没强制传 strategy_key 仍能跑通，但 warning 提示跨 run 聚合查询将无效。

### Files changed

**Added**
- `backend/src/models/RegimeBacktestResult.ts` (new)
- `backend/src/quant/backtest/RegimeSegmentedBacktest.ts` (new)
- `backend/src/scripts/run-regime-backtest.ts` (new)
- `backend/tests/backtest/regime-segmented-backtest.test.ts` (new, 136 ok)

**Modified**
- `backend/src/models/index.ts` (+1 export)
- `backend/src/config/database.ts` (+1 import + 1 model entry)
- `backend/package.json` (+1 npm script `run:regime-backtest`)
- `backend/src/quant/CLAUDE.md` (+'US-040: RegimeSegmentedBacktest' 一节)
- `ralph/prd.json` (US-040 → passes:true + 详细 notes)

  the new modules)

  → **136 ok / 0 failed** (< 50ms)

  - `tests/backtest/walk-forward-validator.test.ts` → 141 ok
  - `tests/backtest/grid-search-optimizer.test.ts` → 100 ok
  - `tests/backtest/bayesian-optimizer.test.ts` → 161 ok
  - `tests/backtest/ashare-constraints.test.ts` → all pass

### Browser verification

**N/A** — backend-only feature；前端 US-016 策略实验室 "环境分段表现" tab
尚未实现 (本 story 是数据 + 算法层)。CLI 端到端 smoke 因本地 DB 没有
backtest results 跳过；生产 deployment 后 ops 可跑 `npm run run:regime-backtest
-- --backtest-result-id=<id> --no-persist` 验证。

### Learnings for future iterations

- **事后分析 vs 优化任务 — 表结构判据 (US-040 关键模式)**: 数据建模的"何时
  新表 vs 何时共享父表"原则。判据：**"这是一个 'task'（有 status 状态机：pending
  → running → completed/failed）还是一个 'snapshot'（对已完成任务的派生统计）？"**
  Task 走 OptimizationRun 共享父表；snapshot 直接通过 FK 引用源 task 的 id。
  盲目新建第 N+1 张 run 表会让 UI 视图 / 状态机 / cleanup 工具被迫支持 N
  种 schema；盲目复用又会让"事后分析"硬塞进 status='completed' 的 task 行
  污染状态机。**未来 US-041 IC report / US-042 correlation / US-043 monte-carlo
  / US-045 benchmark attribution 都是"对已完成 backtest 做派生统计"模式**，
  全部直接引用源 id 不新建 run 表，本 story 是参考。
- **跨模块反向依赖避免：独立实现 vs import (US-040)**: 即便算法完全一致，
  quant/backtest 不 import 自 quant/strategies (会成反向依赖) / 不 import
  自 quant/engine (反向依赖)。**独立实现 + jsdoc 标注"语义 100% 镜像 X 模块的 Y
  函数"** 让 reviewer 一眼看到双地点同步约束。规则边界：根目录 utility
  (utils/stNameUtils.ts 自 US-025 起) 可被多 layer import；layer-specific
  实现 (strategies/mapToEnsembleRegime / engine/QuantMath) 不能跨 layer reach。
- **RegimeSource 接口模式 = WalkForward EmbeddedOptimizer 的副本**: 任何
  "需要查外部状态 (DB / 网络 / 服务) 但又必须可测" 的模块按此 4 步范式 ([1]
  XxxSource 接口 + [2] PRODUCTION_XXX_SOURCE 自带兜底 + [3] options.xxx_source
  默认 PRODUCTION + [4] fake source 通过 Record<key, value> 注入)。**PRODUCTION
  实现必须自己兜底失败** — caller 没办法在抽象 source 接口层加 try/catch
  （每个 source 可能有不同的"什么算失败 / 兜底值是什么"语义）。未来 US-041
  IC report (factor return source) / US-043 monte-carlo (return distribution
  source) / US-046 industry attribution (sector code source) 同款。
- **per-stamp 串行 await 而非 Promise.all 的判据 (US-040)**: 对 200-500 个连
  续日逐日 resolveRegime 时用 `for...of await` 串行而非 `Promise.all`。3
  个理由: (a) MarketEnvironmentService 有 15min 内存 cache (cacheKey 含
  asOfDate)，并发 N 个不同 asOfDate 撞不到 cache 反而 N 倍 DB 查询; (b)
  单测时 fake source 是同步 Map lookup，串/并无差异; (c) 串行天然保持输入
  顺序与输出顺序对齐 (mergeAdjacentSegments 严格依赖 stamps[i] 按 date 升
  序)。**判据**: 上游有 cache / 顺序敏感 / 单次调用快 (< 100ms) 用串行；
  上游真正独立 + 单次慢 (> 500ms) 用 parallel。
- **trade 段归属以 sell_date 为准的 3 个理由 (US-040)**: (a) pnl 在 sell
  时才确定，bull 段买入 + bear 段卖出的 trade，盈亏归属于 bear 段才对应
  "该段实际兑现"; (b) 未平仓 trade (sell_date 未定义) 不计入任何段 — 浮盈
  在 equity_curve 里已经反映，重复计算成 trade 会双计; (c) entry_reason 在
  buy_date 已确定，但段归属看 sell_date 不看 entry_reason，避免"业绩超预期
  入场但行情转熊期卖出"被归错到入场逻辑所属 regime。**未来 US-046 行业归
  因同款判据** (行业归因也以 sell_date 时的行业为准)。
- **JSONB equity_curve 读出时的 Number() 包装 (US-040)**: Sequelize JSONB
  列即便 schema 是 number，从 DB 读出某些字段也可能被序列化成 string (DECIMAL
  类经过 PostgreSQL 协议来回往往这样)。任何 `Number(point.total_value)` 包
  装是 belt-and-suspenders — 已存 number 时 Number() 是 no-op，已存 string
  时正常解析。**规则**: 任何"JSONB 列里包数字" 的读出端 (equity_curve /
  drawdown_curve / metrics_json 里的数值) 都用 Number() 包装，不要相信
  schema 类型。同样适用 US-014 rejected_orders_json 内 reference_price。
- **`mergeAdjacentSegments` 失败模式覆盖 = 单测覆盖率的 6 维**: 空数组 / 单
  天 / 全同 regime / 多 regime 全不同 / 同 regime 不相邻 (bull→bear→bull
  三段不合并) / 4 regime 全出现。看似简单的 run-length encode 算法因"同 regime
  不相邻"判定容易写错 (顺手写成 `if (regime in seen)` 而非 `if (regime ===
  current)`) — 单测显式 5 个 stamps `bull bear bull` 断言 length=3 防止
  退化合并。下次任何 run-length encode 算法测试照搬这 6 维。
- **Sequelize raw query for distinct + group + max 的 escape hatch (US-040
  `listRecentRuns`)**: ORM 不直接支持 "distinct 多列 + count(*) + max(date)
  group by + order"。在不引入巨型 query-builder 依赖的前提下，本地化用
  `sequelize.query(<SQL>, {replacements, type: 'SELECT'})`。**约束**: (a)
  替换值用 `:limit` named replacement 而非字符串拼接防 injection; (b) 返
  回 raw rows 自己 Number() / String() 包装类型 (Postgres 协议给的可能都
  是 string); (c) SQL 注释里说明这是 ORM 不支持的特殊查询 — 让 reviewer
  知道不是懒。下次 US-041 / US-042 需要类似 "list distinct (factor_name,
  look_forward_days) 最近 N 个" 时可复用。
- **136 个测试 < 50ms 的关键 = fake source + in-memory equity_curve fixture**:
  全套测试不碰 DB 不碰 MarketEnvironmentService 不碰真实 RegimeBacktestResult
  写库。`makeEquityCurve(startDate, count, startValue, growthPerDay)` fixture
  避免在每个测试里手写 10 个 QuantEquityPoint 字面量；`growthPerDay=0.01`
  让 sharpe 测试有充足日收益 (≥5 个)，`growthPerDay=0.001` 让短窗口测试
  sharpe=null。**未来任何 backtest 派生模块的测试都按此结构**: fake source
  + in-memory fixture + < 100ms 总耗时。
---


## 2026/06/07 - US-041

### What was implemented

US-041 因子 IC 报告与 IC 衰减分析 — 在 `quant/factors/` 引入了首个**诊断工具**
（不是新因子）`FactorICReport`：对已落库的 FactorScore 与 DailyBar 数据做事后
分析，按 lookForwardDays = 1/5/10/20/60 多窗口计算每日 Spearman 秩相关（IC）
并聚合成 mean / std / IR / positive_ratio 时序统计，把结果落库到 4-tuple
PK `factor_ic_results` 表供策略开发者判断"哪个因子真有 alpha / 哪个该淘汰"。

### Files changed (10)

**新增 4**:
- `backend/src/models/FactorICResult.ts` — 新 Sequelize 模型，4-tuple PK
  (factor_name, look_forward_days, period_start, period_end)；含 ic_mean /
  ic_std / ic_ir / ic_positive_ratio (AC 必须 4 字段) + sample_count /
  universe_avg_size (诊断扩展) + computed_at / source / created_at / updated_at。
  5 个 index 覆盖 factor_name / (factor_name, look_forward_days) /
  look_forward_days / period_end / (factor_name, period_end) 5 种查询模式。
- `backend/src/quant/factors/FactorICReport.ts` (~620 行) — 主模块。导出
  5 个纯函数 (`rankAscending` / `spearmanCorrelation` / `mean` /
  `sampleStddev` / `aggregateICSeries`) + 1 个 class FactorICReport 含
  4 个方法 (generate / getResults / deleteResults / cleanupOlderThan) +
  DataSource 接口注入 (`DefaultFactorICDataSource` + `PRODUCTION_FACTOR_IC_DATA_SOURCE`)。
- `backend/src/scripts/compute-factor-ic.ts` (~280 行) — CLI 含主流程
  (`--factor` / `--factors` / `--start` / `--end` / `--windows` /
  `--universe` / `--no-persist`) + 3 个 admin 模式 (`--list` / `--show` /
  `--cleanup-days`)；退出码 0=全成功 / 1=部分失败但有结果 / 2=严重错误。
- `backend/tests/factors/factor-ic-report.test.ts` (~700 行) — 118 个
  测试 / 全部脱离 DB。覆盖 4 个纯函数 (rankAscending 10 边角 / spearman
  15 边角含 tie/异常值/已知 0.8 数值 / mean+sampleStddev 14 边角 /
  aggregateICSeries 10 边角) + 16 个 end-to-end 场景 (happy 单/多窗口 /
  lookahead bias guard / < MIN cross-section / 空 trade_dates / 自定义
  windows / factor 未注册 ±dataSource / start>=end 抛错 / lookForward<=0
  抛错 / 空 list 抛错 / 非法日期 / 空 factor_name / period_start/end
  字段 / 严格单调 IC=1 / 混合正负聚合 / 真实因子名 / persist 默认 true
  但 sample_count=0 时跳过)。

**修改 6**:
- `backend/src/models/index.ts` — 新增 `export * from ./FactorICResult;`
- `backend/src/config/database.ts` — 新增 import + 注册到 models 数组（让
  `sequelize.sync({alter:true})` 在 dev 模式自动建表）
